# Hall County NE cropland (plain): landscape design report (simulated)

Research software output. Terrain, soils, land cover and climate normals are public data; process rates, prices, species performance and shelter effects are labeled assumptions. A "confirmed (model)" verdict is a statement about the simulations, not a field guarantee.

## Landscape class

- Family: **alley_cropping_windbreak** (slope plain, smoothed grade 1.2%)
- Climate: P 676 mm, PET 1104 mm, P/PET 0.61 (subhumid); 3 months below 0 °C; hardiness zone proxy 5
- Wind: summer from S, winter from NW
- Plain: contour rows are undefined; rows run across the dominant wind and double as snow fences.
- Dryland assumed: irrigation (common on central-plains cropland) is not modeled.

## Tree crops (after Smith, *Tree Crops*)

Eligible: chestnut_hyb (at hardiness limit), hazel_hyb (suitable), walnut_black (suitable), pecan_northern (at hardiness limit), oak_bur (suitable), honeylocust_thornless (suitable), mulberry_red (at hardiness limit), persimmon_am (at hardiness limit), apple_hardy (suitable), saskatoon (suitable), elderberry (suitable), chokecherry (suitable)

Rejected: mulberry_white: invasive-listed in central_plains (listed_some_jurisdictions); needs written override

Nitrogen fixers in the row: buffaloberry

## Search

24 candidates (staged: geometry, then tree-crop mix, then strips), 24 admissible, screened 15 years (central climate, assumed effects). Finalists re-run 40 years under dry/central/wet climate x zero/assumed effects and ranked by their worst case.

| Candidate | Layout | Alley m | Native habitat | Row mix | Screen score | Worst-case score |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| X20 | windbreak | 84 | pollinator understory | food_value | 12,715 | 18,510 |
| X00 | windbreak | 84 | none | food_value | 12,420 | 18,637 |
| X23 | windbreak_winter | 84 | pollinator understory | food_value | 10,686 | 18,516 |
| X02 | windbreak_winter | 84 | none | food_value | 10,373 | — |
| X06 | windbreak | 84 | none | nut_feed_permanent | 9,997 | — |
| X01 | windbreak | 120 | none | food_value | 9,111 | — |
| X07 | windbreak | 84 | none | mixed_food_feed | 8,224 | 11,140 |
| X10 | windbreak_winter | 84 | none | nut_feed_permanent | 7,937 | — |
| X03 | windbreak_winter | 120 | none | food_value | 7,457 | — |
| X18 | windbreak | 84 | grass strips | food_value | 7,425 | — |

Score = NPV vs conventional (2026 USD) + carbon at $50/t on the reversal-adjusted, leakage-net removal (field total).

Price of pollinator habitat (best habitat finalist vs best without, worst case): $121 over 40 years for 0.72 ha (pollinator understory). Pollination gains count only in the assumed-effects runs until measured.

## Chosen design: X23

- Layout windbreak_winter, alley 84 m (7 machinery passes), tree rows 0.72 ha of 12.79 ha; native habitat: pollinator understory
- Pollinator habitat 0.72 ha; 100% of the field within 150 m of it
- Row mix: saskatoon 23%, chestnut_hyb 23%, elderberry 23%, fixers buffaloberry

## Long-term success test: **confirmed (model)**

- PASS S1_carbon_positive_all_draws: reversal-adjusted, leakage-net removal > 0 at the final horizon in every draw
- PASS S2_breakeven_central: cumulative discounted cash vs A turns positive by year 25 (central draw, zero effects)
- PASS S3_establishment_survival: mean establishment survival >= 0.80 in every year and draw (before replanting)
- PASS S4_water_not_worse: surface outflow as a share of water reaching the field not above A by more than 0.5 percentage points in any draw
- PASS S5_integrity: no landscape rule violations; species pass the regional gate
- PASS S6_balances_close: water, carbon and nitrogen balances close in every run

Worst-case net carbon 7.0 tCO2e, best 66.7; breakeven year (central) 11; minimum establishment survival 1.00. Upfront support that would bring breakeven to year 25 (central): $0 ($0 per ha of tree row), the size of a cost-share or pre-purchase agreement that closes the gap.

## Outcomes at year 40 vs conventional (chosen design)

| Draw | Effects | Net carbon tCO2e | Reversal-adj. tCO2e | With food/feed substitution | NPV vs A $ | Outflow change mm | July alley water mm (A) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| dry | zero_effect | 34.0 | 13.4 | 40.8 | 17,844 | -10.2 | -0.0 (-0.0) |
| dry | assumed_effects | 90.8 | 66.7 | 95.1 | 26,960 | -10.6 | -0.0 (-0.0) |
| central | zero_effect | 33.0 | 9.8 | 41.6 | 22,836 | -52.9 | 0.0 (0.0) |
| central | assumed_effects | 84.7 | 57.4 | 93.7 | 32,789 | -53.7 | 1.0 (0.0) |
| wet | zero_effect | 32.1 | 7.0 | 41.9 | 27,186 | -121.0 | 3.4 (3.3) |
| wet | assumed_effects | 68.8 | 42.3 | 79.1 | 36,362 | -121.5 | 13.4 (3.3) |

The substitution column credits tree food/feed against displaced crop output; it is reported, never used as the headline.

## Implementation

- Row area 0.72 ha, 574 plants, replanting allowance 0 (central draw)

| Species | Share of row | Plants | First harvest yr | Full bearing yr |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Saskatoon serviceberry | 0.23 | 134 | 3 | 7 |
| Hybrid chestnut | 0.23 | 134 | 5 | 12 |
| American elderberry | 0.23 | 134 | 2 | 4 |
| Silver buffaloberry | 0.30 | 172 | — | — |

- Year 0, late summer: sow legume cover in the row strips (no tillage of the alleys).
- Year 0, fall: mark rows on the design lines; rip or strip-till row centres only.
- Year 1, spring: plant trees and shrubs into the cover/sod; protect from browse.
- Years 1-3: supplemental water to new plantings in dry months (water is the binding limit here).
- Each season: mow row cover in months [9], blowing clippings to the alleys.
- From year 4: coppice fixers every 4 years; 50% of prunings to on-farm biochar.
- Year 0: sow a native forb understory in the rows instead of clover/vetch; mow once in September after bloom.

## Monitoring

Predictions with envelopes (all draws and effect modes) are in `predictions.csv`; stratified sampling points and the schedule are in `monitoring_plan.json`. Field observations checked with `python3 -m contour.monitor_check` return on_track / watch / off_track per indicator and year.
